mwp_d21_reject_live_roster_effect = {
save_scope_as = mwp_d21j_player
clear_variable_list = mwp_d21j_live_roster
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_domicile value = this }
if = { limit = { exists = domicile } set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_domicile value = domicile }
}
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_count value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_size value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_max value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_current value = 0 }
every_maa_regiment = {
limit = {
 OR = { regiment_owner = scope:mwp_d21j_player AND = { is_title_maa_regiment = yes regiment_owning_title = { holder = scope:mwp_d21j_player } } }
 NOT = { scope:mwp_d21j_player = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = mwp_d21j_live_roster target = prev } } }
}
save_scope_as = mwp_d21j_live_regiment
save_scope_value_as = { name = mwp_d21j_size value = maa_size }
save_scope_value_as = { name = mwp_d21j_max value = maa_max_troops_count }
save_scope_value_as = { name = mwp_d21j_current value = maa_current_troops_count }
scope:mwp_d21j_player = {
 add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_d21j_live_roster target = scope:mwp_d21j_live_regiment }
 change_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_count add = 1 }
 change_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_size add = scope:mwp_d21j_size }
 change_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_max add = scope:mwp_d21j_max }
 change_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_current add = scope:mwp_d21j_current }
}

}
every_held_title = {
every_owned_title_maa_regiment = {
limit = {
 OR = { regiment_owner = scope:mwp_d21j_player AND = { is_title_maa_regiment = yes regiment_owning_title = { holder = scope:mwp_d21j_player } } }
 NOT = { scope:mwp_d21j_player = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = mwp_d21j_live_roster target = prev } } }
}
save_scope_as = mwp_d21j_live_regiment
save_scope_value_as = { name = mwp_d21j_size value = maa_size }
save_scope_value_as = { name = mwp_d21j_max value = maa_max_troops_count }
save_scope_value_as = { name = mwp_d21j_current value = maa_current_troops_count }
scope:mwp_d21j_player = {
 add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_d21j_live_roster target = scope:mwp_d21j_live_regiment }
 change_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_count add = 1 }
 change_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_size add = scope:mwp_d21j_size }
 change_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_max add = scope:mwp_d21j_max }
 change_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_live_current add = scope:mwp_d21j_current }
}

}

}

}
mwp_d21_reject_refresh_effect = {
remove_variable = mwp_d21j_valid
if = { limit = { has_variable = mwp_d21j_context }
 mwp_d21_reject_live_roster_effect = yes
 if = { limit = { mwp_d21_reject_context_current_trigger = yes }
  set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_valid value = yes }
  set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_valid_clock value = var:mwp_d21r_clock }
 }
}

}
mwp_d21_reject_capture_effect = {
remove_variable = mwp_d21j_tx_ready
if = { limit = {
 NOT = { mwp_d21_selected_legacy_rejection_trigger = yes }
 NOT = { mwp_auto_budget_mode_value = 3 }
 has_variable = mwp_enemy_cache_epoch
 mwp_d21_resource_current_trigger = yes
}
 mwp_d21_reject_live_roster_effect = yes
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_owner value = this }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_day value = days_since_birth }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_mode value = mwp_auto_budget_mode_value }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_counter value = mwp_native_cost_purchase_count_value }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_levies value = var:mwp_d21r_max_levies }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_epoch value = var:mwp_enemy_cache_epoch }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_count value = var:mwp_d21j_live_count }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_size value = var:mwp_d21j_live_size }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_max value = var:mwp_d21j_live_max }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_current value = var:mwp_d21j_live_current }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_wallet_1 value = gold }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_wallet_2 value = prestige }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_wallet_3 value = piety }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_wallet_4 value = treasury }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_wallet_5 value = barter_goods }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_balance_1 value = var:mwp_d21r_gold_balance }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_balance_2 value = var:mwp_d21r_prestige_balance }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_balance_3 value = var:mwp_d21r_piety_balance }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_balance_4 value = var:mwp_d21r_treasury_balance }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_balance_5 value = var:mwp_d21r_barter_balance }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_herd_stock value = mwp_d21_reject_herd_stock_value }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_raised value = mwp_d21_reject_raised_value }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_domicile value = var:mwp_d21j_live_domicile }
clear_variable_list = mwp_d21j_tx_roster
every_in_list = { variable = mwp_d21j_live_roster save_scope_as = mwp_d21j_live_regiment
 root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_roster target = scope:mwp_d21j_live_regiment } }
}
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_tx_ready value = yes }
}

}

mwp_d21_reject_record_effect = {
if = { limit = {
 has_variable = mwp_d21j_tx_ready var:mwp_d21j_tx_ready = yes
 has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_refunded var:mwp_d19_tx_refunded = yes
 mwp_d19_transaction_restored_trigger = yes
 NOT = { mwp_d21_selected_legacy_rejection_trigger = yes }
 NOT = { mwp_auto_budget_mode_value = 3 }
}
 save_scope_as = mwp_d21j_player
 if = { limit = { NOT = {
 mwp_d21_reject_same_capture_trigger = yes
 } }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = mwp_d21j_sequence } } set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_sequence value = 0 } }
  change_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_sequence add = 1 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_owner value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_owner }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_day value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_day }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_mode value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_mode }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_counter value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_counter }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_levies value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_levies }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_epoch value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_epoch }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_count value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_count }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_size value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_size }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_max value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_max }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_current value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_current }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_wallet_1 value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_wallet_1 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_wallet_2 value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_wallet_2 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_wallet_3 value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_wallet_3 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_wallet_4 value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_wallet_4 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_wallet_5 value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_wallet_5 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_balance_1 value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_balance_1 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_balance_2 value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_balance_2 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_balance_3 value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_balance_3 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_balance_4 value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_balance_4 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_balance_5 value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_balance_5 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_herd_stock value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_herd_stock }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_raised value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_raised }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context_domicile value = var:mwp_d21j_tx_domicile }

  clear_variable_list = mwp_d21j_context_roster
  every_in_list = { variable = mwp_d21j_tx_roster save_scope_as = mwp_d21j_live_regiment
   root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_d21j_context_roster target = scope:mwp_d21j_live_regiment } }
  }
  set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_context value = yes }
 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_record_nomad_mode value = 0 }
if = { limit = { has_variable = mwp_script_selected_nomad_mode } set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_record_nomad_mode value = var:mwp_script_selected_nomad_mode }
}
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_record_subject_source value = 0 }
if = { limit = { has_variable = mwp_script_selected_subject_source } set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_record_subject_source value = var:mwp_script_selected_subject_source }
}
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_record_herd value = 0 }
if = { limit = { has_variable = mwp_script_selected_herd } set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_record_herd value = var:mwp_script_selected_herd }
}
 set_variable = { name = mwp_d21j_record_route value = mwp_d21_active_route_value }
 if = { limit = { mwp_d21_active_route_value = 2 exists = var:mwp_script_selected_title }
  var:mwp_script_selected_title = { mwp_d21_reject_record_holder_effect = yes }
 }
 else = { mwp_d21_reject_record_holder_effect = yes }
}

}
